- **rust-review-safety**: Reviews Rust code for runtime safety and performance anti-patterns. Checks 3 critical rules: no unwrap()/expect() in production code paths, no blocking operations inside async contexts, and disciplined borrowing over gratuitous cloning. Use this skill whenever reviewing Rust code for reliability, panic-freedom, async correctness, or memory efficiency. Trigger on any Rust review involving error handling, async code, performance, .clone() usage, or production readiness — even if the user just says "is this code production-safe" or "check for panics."
